Key notational setup:
- D = B_1(0) ⊂ ℝ^d, d ≥ 2. 
- u_0(x) = (|x|²−1)/(2d); ∇u_0 = x/d; Δu_0 = 1.
- G(f) = u_f. 
- For h: Λh := w, Δw = −div(h x/d) in D, w|_∂ = 0. So Λh = (−Δ_D)^{-1}[div(hx/d)] = (−Δ_D)^{-1}[(x·∇h + d h)/d]... careful: div(hx/d) = (x·∇h)/d + h·(d/d) = (x·∇h)/d + h ✓.

Final theorem statement:

For d ≥ 2, α > 1, r ∈ (0, r_0]: define the minimax risk ℛ_n. Then for constants C_1, c_1 > 0:
c_1(log n)^{-C_1} n^{-2α/(2α+d+3)} ≤ ℛ_n ≤ C_1(log n)^{C_1} n^{-2α/(2α+d+3)}.
The minimax rate (up to logarithmic factors) is n^{-2α/(2α+d+3)}.

Let me also double-check the lower bound's "small r" requirement once more: Fano needs KL ≤ c log N: KL ≈ n·‖u-diff‖²: computed: ≤ Cnθ²2^{-4j}N·(1+o(1)) with θ²N = sep² = c_0²2^{-2jα}/log^c: KL ≤ Cn c_0²2^{-2jα-4j}/log^c: at j*: 2^{j*} = n^{1/(2α+d+3)}: KL ≤ Cc_0²n^{1 − (2α+4)/(2α+d+3)}/log^c = Cc_0²n^{(d−1)/(2α+d+3)}/log^c: and log N_hyp ≍ N = 2^{j*(d-1)} = n^{(d-1)/(2α+d+3)}: ratio: KL/logN ≤ Cc_0²/log^c → 0 ✓✓ for ANY fixed c_0: no smallness needed for large n!! (The small-r is needed only in the upper bound (linearization) and for the Q-term control (r2^{j(1−α)} ≤ r·n^{(1−α)/(2α+d+3)} → 0 for any fixed r ✓). So r smallness: needed for uniform stability in the upper bound ✓ and stated as in the problem ✓.)

Also the upper bound's uniformity over f_0 ∈ F(α,r): the linearization at f_0 = 1+h_0: the forward map's derivative Λ_{f_0}: its structure: u_{f_0} ≈ u_0: ∇u_{f_0} ≈ x/d: the tangential/good structure persists with uniform constants for r ≤ r_0 ✓ (state as a lemma, cite the structural stability: the critical point of u_{f_0} is nondegenerate near 0, Hopf lemma uniform ✓).
